```tsx
    backend.on("run_thread", () => [done]);
    act(() => backend.emit("runtime", { type: "run_updated", run: done }));
    expect((await screen.findByText("Took")).nextElementSibling).toHaveTextContent("5m 00s");
    expect(screen.queryByText("Running for")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("does not say that a run that waits to start is running", async () => {
    const queued: Run = {
      ...folderRun,
      status: "queued",
      started_at: null,
      ended_at: null,
      summary: null,
    };
    thread([queued]);
    renderApp(`/runs/${run.id}`);
    expect((await screen.findByText("Took")).nextElementSibling).toHaveTextContent("not started");
    expect(screen.queryByText("Running for")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("shows a failure once when the run also stored it as an event", async () => {
    const failed: Run = { ...folderRun, status: "failed", summary: null, error: "engine: boom" };
    thread(
      [failed],
      [
        ev({ kind: "assistant_text", text: "Trying." }),
        ev({ kind: "failed", error: "engine: boom" }),
      ],
    );
    renderApp(`/runs/${run.id}`);
    expect(await screen.findByText("Trying.")).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.getAllByText("engine: boom")).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(screen.getAllByRole("alert")).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("keeps an earlier run's failure in its place, and a stored failure that says something else", async () => {
    const earlier: Run = {
      ...folderRun,
      id: "run-a",
      status: "failed",

    const latest: Run = {

    renderApp("/runs/run-b");
    const sections = await screen.findAllByRole("region", {

    expect(await within(sections[0]).findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("rate limited");
    expect(await within(sections[1]).findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("interrupted");
    // Above the transcript and in the earlier run, not in the last run again.
    expect(screen.getAllByText("rate limited")).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(screen.getAllByRole("alert")).toHaveLength(3);
  });

  it("follows a live run: new events and the new status arrive without a reload", async () => {
    const live: Run = { ...folderRun, status: "running", ended_at: null, summary: null };
    thread([live], [ev({ kind: "assistant_text", text: "Starting." })]);
    renderApp(`/runs/${run.id}`);
    expect(await screen.findByText("Starting.")).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.getByText("Running")).toBeVisible();
    const loads = backend.callsTo("list_run_events").length;

      }),

    // The event was appended, not fetched.

    const done: Run = { ...live, status: "succeeded", ended_at: run.ended_at, summary: "Done." };

    expect(await screen.findByText("Succeeded")).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Cancel run" })).toBeNull();
  });

  it("cancels the run that is still going", async () => {
    const live: Run = { ...folderRun, status: "running", ended_at: null };
    thread([live]);

    const { user, listening } = renderApp(`/runs/${run.id}`);
    const button = await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Mark reviewed" });

    const failed: Run = { ...folderRun, status: "failed", summary: null, error: "timeout" };
    thread([failed]);
    backend.on("mark_reviewed", () => ({ ...failed, reviewed_at: run.ended_at }));
    renderApp(`/runs/${run.id}`);
    const button = await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Mark reviewed" });
    fireEvent.click(button, { detail: 1 });
    expect(await screen.findByText("Marked as reviewed")).toBeVisible();
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Mark reviewed" })).toBeEnabled());
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Mark reviewed" }), { detail: 2 });
    await settle();
    expect(backend.callsTo("mark_reviewed")).toEqual([{ runId: run.id }]);
  });

  it("holds both review actions while one is on its way", async () => {
    thread([run]);
    const answer = later<Run>();
    backend.on("mark_reviewed", () => answer.answer);
    const { user } = renderApp(`/runs/${run.id}`);
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Mark reviewed" }));
    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Discard worktree" })).toBeDisabled(),
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Mark reviewed" })).toBeDisabled();

    act(() => answer.give({ ...run, reviewed_at: run.ended_at }));
    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Discard worktree" })).toBeEnabled(),
    );
  });

  it("marks a failed run as reviewed and shows why it failed", async () => {
    const failed: Run = { ...folderRun, status: "failed", summary: null, error: "timeout" };
    thread([failed]);
    backend.on("mark_reviewed", () => ({ ...failed, reviewed_at: run.ended_at }));
    const { user } = renderApp(`/runs/${run.id}`);
    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("timeout");
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Mark reviewed" }));
    expect(await screen.findByText("Marked as reviewed")).toBeVisible();
    expect(backend.callsTo("mark_reviewed")).toEqual([{ runId: run.id }]);
  });

  it("opens the run's folder, and says so when it is gone", async () => {
    thread([run]);
    backend.on("open_run_folder", () => null);
    const { user } = renderApp(`/runs/${run.id}`);
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Open folder" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(backend.callsTo("open_run_folder")).toEqual([{ runId: run.id }]));

    backend.on("open_run_folder", () => {
      throw { kind: "not_found", message: "the run's folder no longer exists" };
    });
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Open folder" }));
    expect(await screen.findByText("the run's folder no longer exists")).toBeVisible();
  });

  it("asks for a pending approval in place, and sends the decision with its note", async () => {
    const waiting: Run = { ...folderRun, status: "awaiting_approval", ended_at: null };
    thread([waiting], [], [sample.approval]);
    backend.on("decide_approval", () => ({ ...sample.approval, status: "denied" }));
    const { user } = renderApp(`/runs/${run.id}`);
    const card = await screen.findByRole("article", { name: "Approval for Bash" });
    expect(within(card).getAllByText(/git push origin main/)[0]).toBeVisible();
    await user.type(within(card).getByLabelText("Note for the dot"), "not on main");
    await user.click(within(card).getByRole("button", { name: "Deny" }));
    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(backend.callsTo("decide_approval")).toEqual([
        { id: sample.approval.id, approved: false, note: "not on main" },
      ]),
    );
  });

  it("shows a thread of several runs as one conversation", async () => {
    const parent: Run = { ...folderRun, id: "run-a", trigger: "chat", payload: { message: "Fix the bug." } };
    const child: Run = {
      ...folderRun,
      id: "run-b",
      trigger: "resume",
      parent_run_id: "run-a",
      root_run_id: "run-a",
      tokens_in: 10,
      tokens_out: 5,
    };
    thread(
      [{ ...parent, root_run_id: "run-a" }, child],
      [
        ev({ kind: "assistant_text", text: "I need to push." }, "run-a"),
        ev({ kind: "assistant_text", text: "Pushed." }, "run-b"),
      ],
    );
    const { listening } = renderApp("/runs/run-b");
    expect(await screen.findByText("Fix the bug.")).toBeVisible();
    await screen.findByText("Pushed.");
    const sections = screen.getAllByRole("region", { name: /Chat message|Continued after approval/ });
    expect(sections).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(within(sections[0]).getByText("I need to push.")).toBeVisible();
    expect(within(sections[1]).getByText("Pushed.")).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.getByText("1210 in, 345 out")).toBeVisible();
    // The thread is asked for by the run in the address, and again once the app listens.
    await listening();
    expect(backend.callsTo("run_thread")).toEqual([{ runId: "run-b" }, { runId: "run-b" }]);
  });

  it("renders model text without live links, images or raw HTML", async () => {
    thread(
      [folderRun],
      [
        ev({
          kind: "assistant_text",
          text: 'See [the docs](https://example.com/x) ![pic](https://example.com/p.png) <script>alert(1)</script><b id="raw">bold</b>',
        }),
      ],
    );
    renderApp(`/runs/${run.id}`);
    const docs = await screen.findByText("the docs");
    expect(docs.tagName).toBe("SPAN");
    expect(docs).toHaveAttribute("title", "https://example.com/x");
    expect(document.querySelector("main a[href^='http']")).toBeNull();
    expect(document.querySelector("main img")).toBeNull();
    expect(document.querySelector("script")).toBeNull();
    expect(document.getElementById("raw")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("shows a failed load of the run's events", async () => {
    thread([folderRun], [], [sample.approval]);
    backend.on("list_run_events", () => {
      throw { kind: "internal", message: "database is locked" };
    });
    renderApp(`/runs/${run.id}`);
    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("database is locked");
    // What did load is still shown.
    expect(await screen.findByRole("article", { name: "Approval for Bash" })).toBeVisible();
  });

  it("shows a failed load of the run's approvals", async () => {
    thread([folderRun], [ev({ kind: "assistant_text", text: "Starting." })]);
    // An answer this UI cannot read.
    backend.on("approvals_for_run", () => [{ id: 7 }]);
    renderApp(`/runs/${run.id}`);
    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent(/^approvals_for_run: unexpected/);
    expect(await screen.findByText("Starting.")).toBeVisible();
  });

  it("shows one failure once when it stops the same load for every run of the thread", async () => {
    const child: Run = { ...folderRun, id: "run-b", parent_run_id: run.id, trigger: "resume" };
    thread([folderRun, child]);
    backend.on("list_run_events", () => {
      throw { kind: "internal", message: "database is locked" };
    });
    renderApp(`/runs/${run.id}`);
    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("database is locked");
    await waitFor(() => expect(backend.callsTo("list_run_events")).toHaveLength(2));
    expect(screen.getAllByRole("alert")).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("shows why a run could not be loaded", async () => {
    backend.on("run_thread", () => {
      throw { kind: "not_found", message: "run nope" };
    });
    renderApp("/runs/nope");
    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("run nope");
  });
});
```

Create `ui/src/components/ReviewActions.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { commands } from "@/api/commands";
import { ConfirmButton } from "@/components/ConfirmButton";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { notifyError } from "@/lib/notify";
import { invalidate, keys } from "@/lib/query";
import { onSingleClick } from "@/lib/single-click";

/// The two ways a finished run stops waiting for the user: "Mark reviewed", and "Discard
/// worktree", which asks first. The Inbox and the run view show the same two; each says which
/// of them its run can take.
export function ReviewActions(props: {
  runId: string;
  /// Named in the question a discard asks.
  branch: string | null;
  canReview: boolean;
  /// Only when the core says that the worktree exists.
  canDiscard: boolean;
}) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  // What a review changes. Also after a refusal: a run that is gone, or was reviewed or
  // discarded elsewhere, has to show as it is now and leave the Inbox.
  const reload = () => {
    for (const queryKey of [["thread"], ["diff"], keys.inbox]) invalidate(qc, queryKey);
  };
  const markReviewed = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => commands.markReviewed(props.runId),
    onSuccess: () => void toast.success("Marked as reviewed"),
    onError: notifyError,
    onSettled: reload,
  });
  const discard = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => commands.discardWorktree(props.runId),
    onSuccess: () => void toast.success("Worktree discarded"),
    onError: notifyError,
    onSettled: reload,
  });
  const busy = markReviewed.isPending || discard.isPending;
  return (
    <>
      {props.canReview && (
        <Button
          size="sm"
          variant="outline"
          disabled={busy}
          onClick={onSingleClick(() => markReviewed.mutate())}
        >
          Mark reviewed
        </Button>
      )}
      {props.canDiscard && (
        <ConfirmButton
          title="Discard this worktree?"
          description={
            <>
              The worktree folder and the branch <code>{props.branch}</code> are deleted, with
              every change the dot made there. This cannot be undone.
            </>
          }
          confirmLabel="Discard the worktree"
          onConfirm={() => discard.mutate()}
          disabled={busy}
        >
          Discard worktree
        </ConfirmButton>
      )}
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
/// A thread as one conversation: each run of the lineage in order, with its events.
export function Transcript(props: {

  /// an event too: the one of the last run that says the same is left out, so that the error is
```

Replace the contents of `ui/src/screens/RunScreen.tsx` with:

```tsx
import { useMutation, useQueries, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { FolderOpen } from "lucide-react";
import { Link, useParams } from "react-router";

import { commands } from "@/api/commands";
import type { Approval, Run, RunEventRecord } from "@/api/types";
import { ApprovalCard } from "@/components/ApprovalCard";
import { DiffView } from "@/components/DiffView";
import { ErrorNote, LoadErrors } from "@/components/ErrorNote";
import { Markdown } from "@/components/Markdown";
import { Page } from "@/components/Page";
import { ReviewActions } from "@/components/ReviewActions";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/StatusBadge";
import { Transcript } from "@/components/Transcript";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from "@/components/ui/tabs";
import { isActive } from "@/lib/chat-threads";
import { duration, localDateTime } from "@/lib/format";
import { notifyError } from "@/lib/notify";
import { eventsQuery, keys } from "@/lib/query";
import { useNow } from "@/lib/use-now";

/// One thread: the run in the address and every other run of its lineage, as one transcript,
/// with what the thread changed and the actions on it.
function Thread({ runId, runs }: { runId: string; runs: Run[] }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const latest = runs.at(-1)!;
  const active = runs.find(isActive);
  const now = useNow(active !== undefined);
  const dot = useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.dot(latest.dot_id),
    queryFn: () => commands.getDot(latest.dot_id),
  });
  const events = useQueries({ queries: runs.map((run) => eventsQuery(qc, run.id)) });
  const approvals = useQueries({
    queries: runs.map((run) => ({
      queryKey: keys.approvals(run.id),
      queryFn: () => commands.approvalsForRun(run.id),
    })),
  });
  const hasWorktree = latest.branch !== null;
  const diff = useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.diff(runId),
    queryFn: () => commands.worktreeDiff(runId),
    enabled: hasWorktree,
  });

  // The folder is gone once an unchanged worktree was cleaned up or a discard ran; the run keeps
  // its branch name and its path, so only the diff says whether there is still something to
  // discard or to open. Until the diff has answered, neither is known.
  const worktreeKept = diff.data?.exists === true;
  const worktreeGone = diff.data?.exists === false;

  const eventsByRun = new Map<string, RunEventRecord[]>(
    runs.map((run, i) => [run.id, events[i]?.data ?? []]),
  );
  const pending: Approval[] = approvals
    .flatMap((q) => q.data ?? [])
    .filter((a) => a.status === "pending");

  const cancel = useMutation({ mutationFn: commands.cancelRun, onError: notifyError });
  const openFolder = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => commands.openRunFolder(runId),
    onError: notifyError,
  });

  // A diff that failed to load does not say whether a worktree waits to be looked at. The run
  // can be marked all the same, so that a diff that cannot be read does not keep it unreviewed.
  const needsReview =
    !active &&
    latest.reviewed_at === null &&
    (latest.status === "failed" || worktreeKept || diff.isError);
  const tokensIn = runs.reduce((n, r) => n + r.tokens_in, 0);
  const tokensOut = runs.reduce((n, r) => n + r.tokens_out, 0);
  const first = runs[0];
  const took = duration(first.started_at, active ? null : latest.ended_at, now);

  const actions = (
    <>
      <StatusBadge status={latest.status} />
      {active && (
        <Button
          size="sm"
          variant="outline"
          disabled={cancel.isPending}
          onClick={() => cancel.mutate(active.id)}
        >
          Cancel run
        </Button>
      )}
      {latest.workspace_path !== null && !worktreeGone && (
        <Button
          size="sm"
          variant="outline"
          disabled={openFolder.isPending}
          onClick={() => openFolder.mutate()}
        >
          <FolderOpen aria-hidden /> Open folder
        </Button>
      )}
      <ReviewActions
        runId={runId}
        branch={latest.branch}
        canReview={needsReview}
        canDiscard={worktreeKept && !active}
      />
    </>
  );

  const transcript = (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <LoadErrors queries={[...events, ...approvals]} />
      {pending.map((approval) => (
        <ApprovalCard key={approval.id} approval={approval} />
      ))}
      {/* The run's error stands above, so the transcript does not repeat it. */}
      <Transcript runs={runs} events={eventsByRun} shownError={latest.error} />
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <Page title={dot.data ? `${dot.data.name} run` : "Run"} actions={actions}>
      <dl className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-6 gap-y-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        {dot.data && (
          <div className="flex gap-1">
            <dt>Dot</dt>
            <dd>
              <Link to={`/dots/${dot.data.id}`} className="text-foreground hover:underline">
                {dot.data.name}
              </Link>
            </dd>
          </div>
        )}
        <div className="flex gap-1">
          <dt>Queued</dt>
          <dd className="text-foreground">{localDateTime(first.queued_at)}</dd>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-1">
          {/* A thread that waits to start runs for no time yet: it keeps "not started". */}
          <dt>{active && took !== "" ? "Running for" : "Took"}</dt>
          <dd className="text-foreground">{took || "not started"}</dd>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-1">
          <dt>Tokens</dt>
          <dd className="text-foreground">
            {tokensIn} in, {tokensOut} out
          </dd>
        </div>
        {latest.branch && (
          <div className="flex gap-1">
            <dt>Branch</dt>
            <dd className="font-mono text-foreground">{latest.branch}</dd>
          </div>
        )}
      </dl>
      {latest.error && (
        <p role="alert" className="rounded-md border border-destructive/50 p-3 text-destructive">
          {latest.error}
        </p>
      )}
      {latest.summary && (
        <section aria-label="Summary" className="rounded-md border p-3">
          <Markdown text={latest.summary} />
        </section>
      )}
      {hasWorktree ? (
        <Tabs defaultValue="transcript">
          <TabsList>
            <TabsTrigger value="transcript">Transcript</TabsTrigger>
            <TabsTrigger value="changes">Changes</TabsTrigger>
          </TabsList>
          <TabsContent value="transcript">{transcript}</TabsContent>
          <TabsContent value="changes">
            {diff.isError ? (
              <ErrorNote error={diff.error} />
            ) : diff.data && !diff.data.exists ? (
              <p className="text-muted-foreground">
                This run's worktree was removed: it had no changes, or it was discarded.
              </p>
            ) : (
              diff.data && <DiffView diff={diff.data} />
            )}
          </TabsContent>
        </Tabs>
      ) : (
        transcript
      )}
    </Page>
  );
}

export function RunScreen() {
  const { runId = "" } = useParams();
  const thread = useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.thread(runId),
    queryFn: () => commands.runThread(runId),
  });
  if (thread.isPending) return <Page title="Run" />;
  if (thread.isError) {
    return (
      <Page title="Run">
        <ErrorNote error={thread.error} />
      </Page>
    );
  }
  if (thread.data.length === 0) {
    return (
      <Page title="Run">
        <ErrorNote error="This run has no thread." />
      </Page>
    );
  }
  return <Thread key={runId} runId={runId} runs={thread.data} />;
}
```

### Task 9: The editor, the chat, the settings and the Dots list stop blocking or misleading

Places where a screen blocks the user or shows something untrue: a save blocked by a field that is hidden, a chat that can never be continued but looks usable, a refused message that leaves the screen behind the core, a restart that stops running runs without a word, a status event that loses against an older answer.

**Files:**
- Modify: `ui/src/lib/dot-rules.ts`, `ui/src/lib/query.ts`, `ui/src/lib/runtime-events.ts`, `ui/src/app/Banners.tsx`, `ui/src/screens/DotEditorScreen.tsx`, `ui/src/screens/ChatScreen.tsx`, `ui/src/screens/SettingsScreen.tsx`, `ui/src/screens/DotsScreen.tsx`
- Test: `ui/src/lib/dot-rules.test.ts`, `ui/src/screens/DotEditorScreen.test.tsx`, `ui/src/screens/ChatScreen.test.tsx`, `ui/src/screens/SettingsScreen.test.tsx`, `ui/src/screens/DotsScreen.test.tsx`, `ui/src/app/Layout.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 7 `invalidate`, `keys`, `setDotEnabledMutation`, the test harness; Task 8 `isActive`; Task 4's refusals of `chat` (the UI shows their text and does not match on it); `ConfirmButton` (Plan 3b).
- Produces:
  - `mcpOf(spec: DotSpec, mcpText: string): { value: Record<string, unknown> | null } | { error: string }` in `@/lib/dot-rules`: for a local dot it answers `{ value: null }` whatever the hidden field holds. `specErrors` and the editor's save both use it, so what blocks a save and what is saved cannot disagree.
  - Chat: when a thread's latest run has ended without a `session_id`, the composer is disabled with the placeholder `This chat cannot be continued`, and a line under the transcript says `This chat cannot be continued: its last run ended before the dot had a session to resume. Start a new chat.` with a link to `/chat/<dotId>`.
  - Chat: the message box takes the focus when it goes from disabled to enabled, unless the focus is on another control by then.
  - Chat: when the core refuses a follow-up, for any reason, the thread and the dot's lists of runs are loaded again, and the send stays pending until the thread has reloaded. The typed text is kept and the toast shows the core's text.
  - The restart banner restarts at once when no dot has a run that has not ended. Otherwise "Restart Dots" is a `ConfirmButton` (title `Restart Dots now?`, confirm label `Restart now`) that names the dots and says: `A run that is working is stopped by the restart and ends as failed ("interrupted"). Runs that wait in the queue or for an approval continue after it.` It also asks when the list of dots could not be loaded. The Settings screen has no restart control of its own.
  - Settings: editing the program path after a declined confirmation clears "Not saved".
  - Dots list: a row's switch and Run button are disabled only while that dot's own command is pending.
  - `takeStatus(qc: QueryClient, status: Status): void` in `@/lib/query`: stores a status the backend sent and, when a fetch of the status is in flight, drops that fetch for a new one, so an older answer never lands on top. The `status` event and the three commands that answer with a `Status` use it.
  - In the files this task touches, every invalidation that follows a mutation goes through `invalidate`, except the chat's awaited reload of the thread.

```tsx
/// A browser takes the focus from a control that is disabled and leaves it on the page. jsdom
/// keeps it on the control and cannot blur it there, so the focus goes by way of another one.
function loseFocus() {
  const other = screen.getByLabelText("Dot");
  act(() => {
    other.focus();
    other.blur();
  });
}
```

```tsx
  /// A core that holds one more run of thread "a" than the screen does, and so refuses the
  /// screen's next follow-up. `run_thread` answers with that run from the refusal on.
  function behind(newer: Partial<Run>, refusal: string) {
    const runs = chats([chat("a", "10:00")]);
    const thread = { root_run_id: "a", parent_run_id: "a" };
    backend.on("chat", () => {
      if (backend.callsTo("chat").length === 1) {
        runs.push(chat("a2", "10:05", { ...thread, ...newer }));
        throw { kind: "conflict", message: refusal };
      }
      const next = chat("a3", "10:10", { ...thread, parent_run_id: "a2", status: "queued" });
      runs.push(next);
      return next;
    });
    return runs;
  }

  it("loads the thread and the list of chats again when a follow-up is refused", async () => {
    behind(
      { payload: { message: "asked in another window" } },
      "this chat has a newer message than the one you replied to; open the chat again, then send your message",
    );
    const { user, listening } = renderApp(`/chat/${dot.id}/a`);
    const box = await screen.findByLabelText("Message");
    await waitFor(() => expect(box).toBeEnabled());
    await listening();
    const asked = { thread: backend.callsTo("run_thread").length, list: backend.callsTo("list_runs").length };

    await user.type(box, "hello{Enter}");
    expect(await screen.findByText(/this chat has a newer message/)).toBeVisible();
    // The run the screen did not know of is in the transcript, and the text is still there.
    const conversation = within(screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Conversation" }));
    expect(await conversation.findByText("asked in another window")).toBeVisible();
    expect(box).toHaveValue("hello");
    expect(backend.callsTo("run_thread")).toHaveLength(asked.thread + 1);
    expect(backend.callsTo("list_runs")).toHaveLength(asked.list + 1);

    // The next try replies to the run the thread really ends with.
    await waitFor(() => expect(box).toBeEnabled());
    await user.type(box, "{Enter}");
    await waitFor(() => expect(backend.callsTo("chat")).toHaveLength(2));
    expect(backend.callsTo("chat")[1]).toEqual({
      dotId: dot.id,
      message: "hello",
      parentRunId: "a2",
    });
  });

  it("says that the chat cannot be continued when a refusal brings a last run without a session", async () => {
    behind(
      { status: "failed", summary: null, error: "the claude CLI was not found", session_id: null },
      "this chat cannot be continued because its last run ended before the dot started working; start a new chat",
    );
    const { user } = renderApp(`/chat/${dot.id}/a`);
    const box = await screen.findByLabelText("Message");
    await waitFor(() => expect(box).toBeEnabled());
    await user.type(box, "hello{Enter}");

    const conversation = within(screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Conversation" }));
    expect(await conversation.findByText(/This chat cannot be continued/)).toBeVisible();
    expect(conversation.getByRole("link", { name: "Start a new chat" })).toBeVisible();
    expect(box).toBeDisabled();
    expect(box).toHaveValue("hello");
    // No second try goes out.
    await user.type(box, "{Enter}");
    expect(backend.callsTo("chat")).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("keeps the composer locked after a refused follow-up until the thread has reloaded", async () => {
    const runs = behind(
      { status: "running", ended_at: null },
      "the dot is still working on this chat; wait until it has finished, then send your message again",
    );
    const { user } = renderApp(`/chat/${dot.id}/a`);
    const box = await screen.findByLabelText("Message");
    await waitFor(() => expect(box).toBeEnabled());

    // From now on the thread reload is held until the test lets it answer.
    const read = later<Run[]>();
    backend.on("run_thread", () => read.answer);
    await user.type(box, "hello{Enter}");
    expect(await screen.findByText(/the dot is still working on this chat/)).toBeVisible();
    // Until the thread is here the screen still ends at the old run: no second try.
    expect(box).toBeDisabled();
    await user.type(box, "{Enter}");
    expect(backend.callsTo("chat")).toHaveLength(1);

    await act(async () => read.give(runs));
    await waitFor(() => expect(box).toHaveAttribute("placeholder", "The dot is working…"));
    expect(box).toBeDisabled();
    expect(box).toHaveValue("hello");
  });
});
```

```tsx
    "asks before a restart while a run is %s",

      backend.on("restart_app", () => null);

      const dialog = within(await screen.findByRole("alertdialog", { name: "Restart Dots now?" }));
      // The dot whose run has not ended is named, and what the restart does to a working run.

      expect(dialog.getByText(/ends as failed/)).toBeVisible();

  it("asks before a restart once a run has started, and no more when it has ended", async () => {
    let overview = [idle];
    backend.on("status", () => restartOffered);
    backend.on("dot_overview", () => overview);
    backend.on("restart_app", () => null);
    // Settings does not show the dots: only the banner asks for them.
    const { user, listening, queryClient } = renderApp("/settings");
    await restartButton();
    await listening();
    /// Tells the app that the run changed and waits for the list of dots it then asks for.
    async function changed(run: Run) {
      const asked = backend.callsTo("dot_overview").length;
      act(() => backend.emit("runtime", { type: "run_updated", run }));
      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(backend.callsTo("dot_overview")).toHaveLength(asked + 1);
        expect(queryClient.isFetching()).toBe(0);
      });
    }

    const run = active("running");
    overview = [{ ...idle, active_run: run }];
    await changed(run);
    await user.click(await restartButton());
    const dialog = within(await screen.findByRole("alertdialog", { name: "Restart Dots now?" }));
    await user.click(dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Cancel" }));
    expect(backend.callsTo("restart_app")).toEqual([]);

    overview = [idle];
    await changed({ ...run, status: "succeeded" });
    await user.click(await restartButton());
    await waitFor(() => expect(backend.callsTo("restart_app")).toHaveLength(1));
    expect(screen.queryByRole("alertdialog")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("asks before a restart when it cannot tell whether a run is active", async () => {
    backend.on("status", () => restartOffered);
    backend.on("dot_overview", () => {
      throw { kind: "internal", message: "database is locked" };
    });
    backend.on("restart_app", () => null);
    const { user } = renderApp("/settings");
    await user.click(await restartButton());
    const dialog = within(await screen.findByRole("alertdialog", { name: "Restart Dots now?" }));
    expect(dialog.getByText(/could not check whether a run is active/)).toBeVisible();
    expect(backend.callsTo("restart_app")).toEqual([]);
    await user.click(dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Restart now" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(backend.callsTo("restart_app")).toHaveLength(1));
  });

  it("does not ask for the dots' runs while no restart is on offer", async () => {
    const { listening } = renderApp("/settings");
    await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: "Settings" });
    await listening();
    expect(backend.callsTo("dot_overview")).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("loads the Dots list again when schedules were resumed during its first load", async () => {
    let paused = true;
    backend.on("status", () => ({ ...quietStatus, schedules_paused: paused }));
    backend.on("set_schedules_paused", () => {
      paused = false;
      return quietStatus;
    });
    const { user, listening } = renderApp("/settings");
    await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: "Settings" });
    await listening();

    // While paused the core holds the time the dot would have run; a resume moves it on.
    const next = "2026-10-20T01:00:00.000Z";
    const read = later<DotOverview[]>();
    backend.on("dot_overview", () =>
      backend.callsTo("dot_overview").length === 1 ? read.answer : [{ ...idle, next_fire: next }],
    );
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Dots" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(backend.callsTo("dot_overview")).toHaveLength(1));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Resume schedules" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByText(/Schedules are paused/)).toBeNull());
    // The list was read before the resume.
    await act(async () => read.give([idle]));

    expect(await screen.findByText(localDateTime(next))).toBeVisible();
    expect(backend.callsTo("dot_overview")).toHaveLength(2);
  });
```

```tsx
  it("takes a status that changed outside the window", async () => {
    const { listening } = renderApp("/dots");
    await screen.findByRole("navigation", { name: "Main" });
    await listening();
    const asked = backend.callsTo("status").length;
    act(() => backend.emit("status", { ...quietStatus, schedules_paused: true }));
    expect(await screen.findByText(/Schedules are paused/)).toBeVisible();
    // No question was on its way, so the status that arrived is the newest: nothing is asked.
    expect(backend.callsTo("status")).toHaveLength(asked);
  });

  it("keeps the status the tray sent while an older answer was on its way", async () => {
    const paused = { ...quietStatus, schedules_paused: true };
    const attach = backend.holdListeners();
    // The first answer comes at once, the second when the test gives it, later ones say what
    // the core holds by then: paused.
    const read = later<Status>();
    backend.on("status", () => {
      const asked = backend.callsTo("status").length;
      return asked === 1 ? quietStatus : asked === 2 ? read.answer : paused;
    });
    const { queryClient } = renderApp("/dots");
    await screen.findByRole("navigation", { name: "Main" });
    await waitFor(() => expect(queryClient.isFetching()).toBe(0));

    // Once the app listens it asks for everything again. The core reads the status for that
    // answer, then the tray pauses the schedules and says so before the answer is here.
    await act(async () => attach());
    await waitFor(() => expect(backend.callsTo("status")).toHaveLength(2));
    act(() => backend.emit("status", paused));
    expect(await screen.findByText(/Schedules are paused/)).toBeVisible();
    await act(async () => read.give(quietStatus));
    await waitFor(() => expect(queryClient.isFetching()).toBe(0));

    expect(screen.getByText(/Schedules are paused/)).toBeVisible();
    expect(queryClient.getQueryData(keys.status)).toEqual(paused);
    // The answer on its way was dropped for one read after the change.
    expect(backend.callsTo("status")).toHaveLength(3);
  });
```

```ts
}

/// Takes a status the backend sent: the tray's event, or the answer of a command that changed
/// it. A question about the status that is on its way may have been answered before that
/// change, and its answer would replace this status. So that question is dropped for one asked
/// now: marking a query that has data and is on screen stale starts its fetch again.
export function takeStatus(qc: QueryClient, status: Status): void {
  const asking = qc.getQueryState(keys.status)?.fetchStatus === "fetching";
  qc.setQueryData(keys.status, status);
  if (asking) invalidate(qc, keys.status);
}
```

```tsx
/// "Restart Dots". With no run that has not ended it restarts at once. Otherwise it asks
/// first: `active` names the dots that have such a run, and is `undefined` when that is not
/// known, which asks too.
function RestartButton(props: {
  active: string[] | undefined;
  disabled: boolean;
  onRestart: () => void;
}) {
  const { active } = props;
  if (active?.length === 0) {
    return (
      <Button size="sm" variant="outline" disabled={props.disabled} onClick={props.onRestart}>
        Restart Dots
      </Button>
    );
  }
  const found = !active
    ? "Dots could not check whether a run is active."
    : active.length === 1
      ? `${active[0]} has a run that has not ended.`
      : `${active.join(", ")} have runs that have not ended.`;
  return (
    <ConfirmButton
      title="Restart Dots now?"
      description={
        <>
          {found} A run that is working is stopped by the restart and ends as failed
          ("interrupted"). Runs that wait in the queue or for an approval continue after it.
        </>
      }
      confirmLabel="Restart now"
      onConfirm={props.onRestart}
      disabled={props.disabled}
    >
      Restart Dots
    </ConfirmButton>
  );
}
```

Replace the contents of `ui/src/screens/ChatScreen.tsx` with:

```tsx
import { useMutation, useQueries, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { cn } from "cn";
import { Plus, Send } from "lucide-react";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router";

import { commands } from "@/api/commands";
import type { Run, RunEventRecord } from "@/api/types";
import { ApprovalCard } from "@/components/ApprovalCard";
import { ErrorNote, LoadErrors } from "@/components/ErrorNote";
import { Page } from "@/components/Page";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/StatusBadge";
import { Transcript } from "@/components/Transcript";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { NativeSelect } from "@/components/ui/native-select";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { chatThreads, isActive } from "@/lib/chat-threads";
import { relativeTime } from "@/lib/format";
import { notifyError } from "@/lib/notify";
import { eventsQuery, invalidate, keys } from "@/lib/query";

/// Where a message is typed. Enter sends; Shift+Enter starts a new line. The text is cleared
/// once the send has succeeded; a refused message stays for another try.
function Composer(props: {
  disabled: boolean;
  hint: string;
  onSend: (message: string) => Promise<unknown>;
}) {
  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const box = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);
  // The box is disabled while a send is on its way and while the dot works, and a browser
  // takes the focus from a disabled control. When the box can be used again it gets the focus
  // back, unless the user has put it somewhere else in the meantime.
  const wasDisabled = useRef(props.disabled);
  useEffect(() => {
    const freed = wasDisabled.current && !props.disabled;
    wasDisabled.current = props.disabled;
    const focused = document.activeElement;
    if (freed && (focused === null || focused === document.body || focused === box.current)) {
      box.current?.focus();
    }
  }, [props.disabled]);
  function send() {
    const message = text.trim();
    if (message === "" || props.disabled) return;
    // The mutation's onError reports a failure; here it only keeps the text.
    props.onSend(message).then(
      () => setText(""),
      () => {},
    );
  }
  return (
    <div className="flex items-end gap-2 border-t p-3">
      <label htmlFor="chat-message" className="sr-only">
        Message
      </label>
      <Textarea
        id="chat-message"
        ref={box}
        rows={2}
        placeholder={props.hint}
        disabled={props.disabled}
        value={text}
        onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
        onKeyDown={(e) => {
          if (e.key === "Enter" && !e.shiftKey) {
            e.preventDefault();
            send();
          }
        }}
      />
      <Button disabled={props.disabled || text.trim() === ""} onClick={send}>
        <Send aria-hidden /> Send
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}

/// One conversation: its runs as a transcript, approvals it waits for, and the composer.
function Conversation({ dotId, rootId }: { dotId: string; rootId: string }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const thread = useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.thread(rootId),
    queryFn: () => commands.runThread(rootId),
  });
  const runs: Run[] = thread.data ?? [];
  const events = useQueries({ queries: runs.map((run) => eventsQuery(qc, run.id)) });
  const approvals = useQueries({
    queries: runs.map((run) => ({
      queryKey: keys.approvals(run.id),
      queryFn: () => commands.approvalsForRun(run.id),
    })),
  });
  const latest = runs.at(-1);
  // A follow-up continues from the thread's last run, so it has to have ended.
  const busy = runs.some(isActive);
  // And it resumes that run's session. A run that ended before its engine had one (a failed
  // start, a cancel while queued) leaves nothing to resume: the core refuses every follow-up.
  const closed = !busy && latest !== undefined && latest.session_id === null;
  // Loads the dot's chats and the thread again. The thread's promise is returned for the send
  // to wait on. That needs the promise `invalidate` does not give, and not its care for a
  // fetch in flight: the thread is on screen with its runs, so a refetch on its way is started
  // again.
  const reload = () => {
    invalidate(qc, ["runs"]);
    return qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["thread"] });
  };
  const send = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (message: string) => commands.chat(dotId, message, latest!.id),
    // Returned, so the send stays pending until the thread has reloaded: until then the
    // thread still ends at the old run, and a second message would continue from it.
    onSuccess: reload,
    // The core refuses a follow-up when the thread it holds is ahead of this one: a newer
    // message, a run that still works or waits for an approval, a last run without a session.
    // So the thread is loaded again after any refusal, and the composer follows what it brings;
    // until then a second try would only be refused the same way.
    onError: (error) => {
      notifyError(error);
      return reload();
    },
  });
  if (thread.isPending) return null;
  if (thread.isError) return <ErrorNote error={thread.error} />;
  const eventsByRun = new Map<string, RunEventRecord[]>(
    runs.map((run, i) => [run.id, events[i]?.data ?? []]),
  );
  const pending = approvals.flatMap((q) => q.data ?? []).filter((a) => a.status === "pending");
  // Why the last run failed stands below the conversation, so the transcript does not repeat it.
  const failure = latest?.status === "failed" ? latest.error : null;
  return (
    <>
      <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-4 overflow-auto p-4">
        <Transcript runs={runs} events={eventsByRun} shownError={failure} />
        <LoadErrors queries={[...events, ...approvals]} />
        {pending.map((approval) => (
          <ApprovalCard key={approval.id} approval={approval} />
        ))}
        {failure && (
          <p role="alert" className="rounded-md border border-destructive/50 p-2 text-destructive">
            {failure}
          </p>
        )}
        {closed && (
          <p className="text-muted-foreground">
            This chat cannot be continued: its last run ended before the dot had a session to
            resume.{" "}
            <Link to={`/chat/${dotId}`} className="underline">
              Start a new chat
            </Link>
            .
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
      <Composer
        disabled={busy || send.isPending || !latest || closed}
        hint={
          busy
            ? "The dot is working…"
            : closed
              ? "This chat cannot be continued"
              : "Reply to continue this chat"
        }
        onSend={(message) => send.mutateAsync(message)}
      />
    </>
  );
}

/// A new conversation: the first message starts a chat run, which becomes the thread.
function NewConversation({ dotId }: { dotId: string }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const start = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (message: string) => commands.chat(dotId, message, null),
    onSuccess: (run) => {
      invalidate(qc, ["runs"]);
      void navigate(`/chat/${dotId}/${run.id}`);
    },
    onError: notifyError,
  });
  return (
    <>
      <p className="flex-1 p-4 text-muted-foreground">
        Ask the dot something. It answers with its own instructions, folder and permissions.
      </p>
      <Composer
        disabled={start.isPending}
        hint="Message the dot"
        onSend={(message) => start.mutateAsync(message)}
      />
    </>
  );
}

function Threads({ dotId, rootId }: { dotId: string; rootId: string | undefined }) {
  const runs = useQuery({
    // Its own key under "runs": the dot editor lists the same dot's runs with another limit.
    queryKey: [...keys.runs(dotId), "chat"],
    queryFn: () => commands.listRuns(dotId, 200),
  });
  const threads = chatThreads(runs.data ?? []);
  return (
    <nav aria-label="Chats" className="flex flex-col gap-1">
      <Button asChild size="sm" variant="outline">
        <Link to={`/chat/${dotId}`}>
          <Plus aria-hidden /> New chat
        </Link>
      </Button>
      {runs.isError && <ErrorNote error={runs.error} />}
      {threads.map((thread) => (
        <Link
          key={thread.rootId}
          to={`/chat/${dotId}/${thread.rootId}`}
          aria-current={thread.rootId === rootId ? "page" : undefined}
          className={cn(
            "flex flex-col gap-0.5 rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 hover:bg-accent",
            thread.rootId === rootId && "bg-accent",
          )}
        >
          <span className="truncate">{thread.title}</span>
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            {isActive(thread.latest) && <StatusBadge status={thread.latest.status} />}
            {relativeTime(thread.latest.queued_at)}
          </span>
        </Link>
      ))}
    </nav>
  );
}

/// Chat with a dot: pick the dot, then one of its conversations or a new one.
export function ChatScreen() {
  const { dotId, runId } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const dots = useQuery({ queryKey: keys.dots, queryFn: commands.listDots });
  if (dots.isPending) return <Page title="Chat" />;
  if (dots.isError) {
    return (
      <Page title="Chat">
        <ErrorNote error={dots.error} />
      </Page>
    );
  }
  if (dots.data.length === 0) {
    return (
      <Page title="Chat">
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">
          There is no dot to chat with yet. <Link to="/dots/new" className="underline">Create one</Link>.
        </p>
      </Page>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full">
      <aside className="flex w-60 shrink-0 flex-col gap-3 border-r p-3">
        <h1 className="text-lg font-semibold">Chat</h1>
        <label htmlFor="chat-dot" className="sr-only">
          Dot
        </label>
        <NativeSelect
          id="chat-dot"
          className="w-full"
          value={dotId ?? ""}
          onChange={(e) => void navigate(e.target.value ? `/chat/${e.target.value}` : "/chat")}
        >
          <option value="">Choose a dot…</option>
          {dots.data.map((dot) => (
            <option key={dot.id} value={dot.id}>
              {dot.name}
            </option>
          ))}
        </NativeSelect>
        {dotId && <Threads dotId={dotId} rootId={runId} />}
      </aside>
      <section aria-label="Conversation" className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col">
        {!dotId ? (
          <p className="p-4 text-muted-foreground">Choose a dot to chat with.</p>
        ) : runId ? (
          <Conversation key={runId} dotId={dotId} rootId={runId} />
        ) : (
          <NewConversation key={dotId} dotId={dotId} />
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

Replace the contents of `ui/src/screens/SettingsScreen.tsx` with:

```tsx
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Plus, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { CommandError, commands } from "@/api/commands";
import { LIMITS, type Settings, type Status } from "@/api/types";
import { ErrorNote } from "@/components/ErrorNote";
import { Field } from "@/components/Field";
import { Page } from "@/components/Page";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
import { errorMessage, notifyError } from "@/lib/notify";
import { invalidate, keys, takeStatus } from "@/lib/query";

type Errors = { port?: string; max?: string };

/// What is wrong with the numbers, mirroring `Settings::validate` in the core.
export function settingsErrors(draft: Pick<Settings, "port" | "max_concurrent_runs">): Errors {
  const errors: Errors = {};
  const whole = (n: number, min: number, max: number) => Number.isInteger(n) && n >= min && n <= max;
  if (!whole(draft.port, 1, 65535)) errors.port = "The port must be a whole number, 1 to 65535.";
  if (!whole(draft.max_concurrent_runs, 1, LIMITS.max_concurrent_runs)) {
    errors.max = `Runs at once must be a whole number, 1 to ${LIMITS.max_concurrent_runs}.`;
  }
  return errors;
}

function Section(props: { title: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <fieldset className="flex max-w-3xl flex-col gap-3 rounded-lg border p-4">
      <legend className="px-1 text-sm font-semibold">{props.title}</legend>
      {props.children}
    </fieldset>
  );
}

const numberOf = (text: string) => (text === "" ? Number.NaN : Number(text));
const shown = (n: number) => (Number.isNaN(n) ? "" : n);

/// One local endpoint: remove it, or ask it for a tool call to see that it works.
function Endpoint(props: { url: string; onRemove: () => void }) {
  const [model, setModel] = useState("");
  const test = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => commands.testEndpoint(props.url, model.trim()),
  });
  return (
    <li aria-label={props.url} className="flex flex-col gap-1.5 rounded-md border p-2">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate font-mono text-xs">{props.url}</span>
        <Input
          aria-label={`Model for ${props.url}`}
          placeholder="Model to test with"
          className="w-56"
          value={model}
          onChange={(e) => setModel(e.target.value)}
        />
        <Button
          type="button"
          size="sm"
          variant="outline"
          disabled={test.isPending || model.trim() === ""}
          onClick={() => test.mutate()}
        >
          {test.isPending ? "Testing…" : "Test endpoint"}
        </Button>
        <Button
          type="button"
          size="icon-sm"
          variant="ghost"
          aria-label={`Remove ${props.url}`}
          onClick={props.onRemove}
        >
          <Trash2 aria-hidden />
        </Button>
      </div>
      {test.isSuccess && (
        <p role="status" className="text-xs text-emerald-300">
          {test.data}
        </p>
      )}
      {test.isError && (
        <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-destructive">
          {errorMessage(test.error)}
        </p>
      )}
    </li>
  );
}

function Form({ stored, status }: { stored: Settings; status: Status | undefined }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(stored);
  const [claudePath, setClaudePath] = useState(stored.claude_path ?? "");
  const [newEndpoint, setNewEndpoint] = useState("");
  const [endpointError, setEndpointError] = useState<string>();
  const [tried, setTried] = useState(false);
  const set = (change: Partial<Settings>) => setDraft({ ...draft, ...change });

  const save = useMutation({
    mutationFn: commands.updateSettings,
    onSuccess: (saved) => {
      qc.setQueryData(keys.settings, saved.settings);
      // The restart banner comes from the status.
      invalidate(qc, keys.status);
      toast.success(saved.restart_required ? "Saved. Restart Dots to apply." : "Saved");
    },
  });
  // Declining the native confirmation is the user's own answer, not a failure.
  const declined = save.error instanceof CommandError && save.error.kind === "cancelled";
  const pause = useMutation({
    mutationFn: commands.setSchedulesPaused,
    onSuccess: (next) => {
      takeStatus(qc, next);
      // A pause or a resume moves every schedule's next run, which the Dots list holds.
      invalidate(qc, keys.overview);
    },
    onError: notifyError,
  });
  const markTested = useMutation({
    mutationFn: commands.markClaudeTested,
    onSuccess: (next) => takeStatus(qc, next),
    onError: notifyError,
  });

  const errors = settingsErrors(draft);
  function submit() {
    setTried(true);
    if (Object.keys(errors).length > 0) return;
    save.mutate({ ...draft, claude_path: claudePath.trim() || null });
  }
  function addEndpoint() {
    const url = newEndpoint.trim();
    if (!/^http:\/\/[^\s/]+/.test(url)) {
      setEndpointError("An endpoint starts with http:// and names a host.");
      return;
    }
    if (!draft.local_endpoints.includes(url)) {
      set({ local_endpoints: [...draft.local_endpoints, url] });
    }
    setNewEndpoint("");
    setEndpointError(undefined);
  }

  const claude = status?.claude;
  return (
    <Page
      title="Settings"
      actions={
        <Button size="sm" disabled={save.isPending} onClick={submit}>
          Save
        </Button>
      }
    >
      {save.isError && !declined && <ErrorNote error={save.error} />}

      <Section title="General">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Switch
            id="settings-autostart"
            checked={draft.autostart}
            onCheckedChange={(autostart) => set({ autostart })}
          />
          <Label htmlFor="settings-autostart">Start Dots when I sign in</Label>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Switch
            id="settings-paused"
            checked={status?.schedules_paused ?? false}
            disabled={!status || pause.isPending}
            onCheckedChange={(paused) => pause.mutate(paused)}
          />
          <Label htmlFor="settings-paused">Pause all schedules</Label>
        </div>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          Pausing applies at once and is not part of Save. Webhooks, "Run now" and chat still
          work while schedules are paused.
        </p>
        <Field label="Data folder" htmlFor="settings-data-dir">
          <Input
            id="settings-data-dir"
            readOnly
            className="font-mono"
            value={status?.data_dir ?? ""}
          />
        </Field>
      </Section>

      <Section title="Local server and runs">
        <Field
          label="Port"
          htmlFor="settings-port"
          error={tried ? errors.port : undefined}
          hint="Webhooks and the Claude CLI's permission prompts reach Dots on 127.0.0.1 at this port. Takes effect after a restart."
        >
          <Input
            id="settings-port"
            type="number"
            className="w-40"
            value={shown(draft.port)}
            onChange={(e) => set({ port: numberOf(e.target.value) })}
          />
        </Field>
        <Field
          label="Runs at once"
          htmlFor="settings-max"
          error={tried ? errors.max : undefined}
          hint="More runs wait in the queue. Takes effect after a restart."
        >
          <Input
            id="settings-max"
            type="number"
            className="w-40"
            value={shown(draft.max_concurrent_runs)}
            onChange={(e) => set({ max_concurrent_runs: numberOf(e.target.value) })}
          />
        </Field>
      </Section>

      <Section title="Claude Code CLI">
        <dl className="grid grid-cols-[max-content_1fr] gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-xs">
          <dt className="text-muted-foreground">In use</dt>
          <dd className="font-mono">{claude?.program ?? "not found"}</dd>
          <dt className="text-muted-foreground">Version</dt>
          <dd>{claude?.version ?? "unknown"}</dd>
          <dt className="text-muted-foreground">Tested with</dt>
          <dd>{claude?.tested_version ?? ""}</dd>
        </dl>
        {claude?.mismatch && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <p className="flex-1 text-xs text-amber-300">
              This version has not been tested with Dots. If your dots run as expected, mark it
              as tested.
            </p>
            <Button
              type="button"
              size="sm"
              variant="outline"
              disabled={markTested.isPending}
              onClick={() => markTested.mutate()}
            >
              Mark as tested
            </Button>
          </div>
        )}
        <Field
          label="Program path"
          htmlFor="settings-claude-path"
          error={declined ? "Not saved: you declined the change of the Claude program." : undefined}
          hint="Leave empty to find claude on your PATH. A new program must be confirmed in a system dialog, and takes effect after a restart."
        >
          <Input
            id="settings-claude-path"
            className="font-mono"
            value={claudePath}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setClaudePath(e.target.value);
              // "Not saved" is about the path that was declined, not about this one.
              if (declined) save.reset();
            }}
          />
        </Field>
      </Section>

      <Section title="Local endpoints">
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          OpenAI-compatible servers that local dots can use, such as LM Studio or Ollama. Any
          http:// host is accepted, so a model server on your network works too. Requests carry
          your dots' instructions and file contents: list only servers you trust.
        </p>
        {draft.local_endpoints.length > 0 && (
          <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            {draft.local_endpoints.map((url) => (
              <Endpoint
                key={url}
                url={url}
                onRemove={() =>
                  set({ local_endpoints: draft.local_endpoints.filter((u) => u !== url) })
                }
              />
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
        <Field label="Add an endpoint" htmlFor="settings-new-endpoint" error={endpointError}>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <Input
              id="settings-new-endpoint"
              className="font-mono"
              placeholder="http://127.0.0.1:1234"
              value={newEndpoint}
              onChange={(e) => setNewEndpoint(e.target.value)}
              onKeyDown={(e) => {
                if (e.key === "Enter") addEndpoint();
              }}
            />
            <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={addEndpoint}>
              <Plus aria-hidden /> Add
            </Button>
          </div>
        </Field>
      </Section>
    </Page>
  );
}

export function SettingsScreen() {
  const settings = useQuery({ queryKey: keys.settings, queryFn: commands.getSettings });
  const status = useQuery({ queryKey: keys.status, queryFn: commands.status }).data;
  if (settings.isPending) return <Page title="Settings" />;
  if (settings.isError) {
    return (
      <Page title="Settings">
        <ErrorNote error={settings.error} />
      </Page>
    );
  }
  return <Form stored={settings.data} status={status} />;
}
```

### Task 10: The installer

`tauri build` already makes an NSIS installer. This task writes the spec's two choices into the configuration so that they stay put if a default changes, builds the installer once, and reads the generated script to confirm what it will do on a user's machine. Nobody runs the installer in this task.

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/dots-app/tauri.conf.json`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the finished app; the `tauri` script of the root `package.json`; the icons under `apps/dots-app/icons/` (complete, made in Plan 3a; `tauri icon` is not run again).
- Produces:
  - `bundle.windows.nsis.installMode: "currentUser"` and `bundle.windows.webviewInstallMode: { "type": "downloadBootstrapper", "silent": true }`. Both are Tauri's defaults today. The key names and values come from `node_modules/@tauri-apps/cli/config.schema.json` (CLI 2.12.1).
  - The installer `target/release/bundle/nsis/Dots_0.1.0_x64-setup.exe` (about 5.3 MiB, unsigned). It is not committed.

What the generated script (`target/release/nsis/x64/installer.nsi`) shows, read and not run:

- The install is per user and asks for no elevation: `!define INSTALLMODE "currentUser"`, `RequestExecutionLevel user`; the uninstall entry is under `HKCU`.
- It installs into `$LOCALAPPDATA\${PRODUCTNAME}`, which is `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Dots`. **That is the same folder as Dots' data folder, `%LOCALAPPDATA%\dots`: Windows ignores the case.** After an install, `dots-app.exe` and `uninstall.exe` lie next to the database, the settings, the logs and the worktrees.
- The uninstaller deletes `dots-app.exe` and `uninstall.exe`, then `RMDir "$INSTDIR"` without `/r`, which removes the folder only when it is empty. So the data stays, as the spec asks.
- The uninstaller's box "Delete the application data" is unticked by default. Ticked, it removes `%APPDATA%\dev.dots.app` and `%LOCALAPPDATA%\dev.dots.app` (the webview's profile). It never removes `%LOCALAPPDATA%\dots`.
- When WebView2 is missing, the installer downloads Microsoft's bootstrapper and stops with an error if it cannot. Windows 11 has WebView2.
- A release build has no `DOTS_CONFIRM` bypass: no profile sets `debug-assertions`, so `cfg!(debug_assertions)` is false, and `confirm::tests::only_debug_builds_take_the_answer_from_the_environment` holds the rule.
